# Skills · R · part 30

868 entries, retro to reverse-engineer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **retro**: Periodic re-read of recent shipped work against the product principles (CLAUDE.md), .claude/rules/plans.md, and .claude/rules/mandate.md. Catches plan-format drift, intent drift, workflow drift, and rules that didn't fire when they should have. Use every 2 weeks, after archiving 3+ plans, or when the user calls "let's retro." Outputs: a list of rule deltas to commit, or "clean — no drift found. ([jonaseck2/slaktforskning](https://github.com/jonaseck2/slaktforskning/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/retro) · ★2)
- **retro**: Post-session retrospective focused on process, not code — what shipped, what cost time, and specific actionable improvements for future sessions. Use at the END of a session to capture lessons; distinct from /checkpoint (mid-session status) and /pickup (resuming a prior session). Brief by design — surface only what matters. ([jorjani/product-squad](https://github.com/jorjani/product-squad/tree/HEAD/skills/retro) · ★2)
- **retro**: 改善サイクルの有効性を検証する。改善記録の効果測定、プロジェクト健全性のトレンド分析、SRE 的観点での運用改善を実施する。 ([ka2kama/ringiflow](https://github.com/ka2kama/ringiflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/retro) · ★2)
- **retro**: Run a sprint retrospective on evidence rather than memory, and write the status report that goes up: what the data says about the sprint, what the team says, the few actions worth taking with an owner each, and whether last retro's actions actually happened. Use at the end of a sprint, a milestone, or a phase, and when a status report is due to a PM or a client. Triggers on: "retro", "retrospective", "họp retro", "tổng kết sprint", "sprint review", "status report", "振り返り", "レトロスペクティブ", "weekly report", "/atk:retro". ([lamngockhuong/aiteamkit](https://github.com/lamngockhuong/aiteamkit/tree/HEAD/skills/retro) · ★2)

([Lengros/bennu](https://github.com/Lengros/bennu/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/retro) · ★2)
- **retro**: Harvest durable learnings from this session into the instruction system — with provenance, dedup, and pruning.
